# Prado-origin reproduction decisions

These 3188 selected image files originate from Prado. Commons labels and museum reproduction terms are preserved separately. No independent commercial permission has been obtained. Current Artline public/commercial policy holds unresolved permission cases.

Museum terms: https://www.museodelprado.es/en/legal-information
